    new_session=one(c,'SELECT * FROM plugin_sessions WHERE plugin_session_id=?',(session_id,));audit_in_tx(c,a,'revoke','plugin_session',session_id,old_session,new_session,reason,dev.get('workspace_id'))
   return h.sendj({'ok':True,'item':new_session}) or True
  if ident and action=='history' and m=='GET':
   if not (platform(db,a) or can(db,a,'device.read')):return bad(h,'FORBIDDEN','无设备读取权限',403)

    if not allow_raw:return bad(h,'FORBIDDEN','无消息原文读取权限',403)

   messages=rows(c,f"SELECT m.message_id,m.conversation_id,m.direction,m.sender_type,m.content_type,CASE WHEN m.content_deleted_at IS NOT NULL THEN NULL ELSE m.{text_column} END AS text,m.occurred_at,m.collected_at,m.status,m.content_deleted_at,m.metadata_json,ca.display_name channel_account_name,p.platform_name platform_name FROM conversation_messages m LEFT JOIN conversations v ON v.conversation_id=m.conversation_id LEFT JOIN channel_accounts ca ON ca.channel_account_id=v.channel_account_id LEFT JOIN platforms p ON p.platform_id=v.platform_id WHERE m.contact_id=?     m['metadata']=safe_meta
   except Exception:m['metadata']={}
   if allow_raw:audit_in_tx(c,a,'read_raw','contact_messages',contact_id,{}, {'contactId':contact_id,'workspaceId':r.get('workspace_id'),'teamId':r.get('team_id'),'scope':'latest_10'},'explicit raw message access',r.get('workspace_id'))
  return h.sendj({'ok':True,'contact':r,'auditLogs':logs,'touchpoints':touchpoints,'messages':messages,'messageContent':'raw' if allow_raw else 'redacted'}) or True

 # audit
 if root=='audit' and m=='GET':
  if not (platform(db,a) or can(db,a,'audit.read')):return bad(h,'FORBIDDEN','无审计读取权限',403)
  pno,size,off=page(q);et=q.get('entity_type');op=q.get('operation');uid=q.get('user_id');eid=q.get('entity_id');cond='u.organization_id=?';args=[org]
  if et:cond+=' AND l.entity_type=?';args.append(et)
  if op:cond+=' AND l.operation=?';args.append(op)
  if uid:cond+=' AND l.actor_id=?';args.append(uid)
  if eid:cond+=' AND l.entity_id=?';args.append(eid)
  if q.get('days'):cond+=' AND l.created_at>=?';args.append(now_ms()-int(q['days'])*86400*1000)
  with db.tx(False) as c:
   total=c.execute(f'SELECT count(*) FROM audit_logs l JOIN users u ON u.user_id=l.actor_id WHERE {cond}',tuple(args)).fetchone()[0]
   its=rows(c,f'SELECT l.*,u.display_name actor_name FROM audit_logs l JOIN users u ON u.user_id=l.actor_id WHERE {cond} ORDER BY audit_id DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?',tuple(args+[size,off]))
   since_ms=now_ms()-30*86400*1000
   heat=rows(c,f"SELECT cast(strftime('%w',l.created_at/1000,'unixepoch','localtime') as int) weekday,cast(strftime('%H',l.created_at/1000,'unixepoch','localtime') as int) hour,count(*) cnt FROM audit_logs l JOIN users u ON u.user_id=l.actor_id WHERE l.created_at>=? AND u.organization_id=? GROUP BY weekday,hour",(since_ms,org))
   op_dist=rows(c,f"SELECT operation name,count(*) value FROM audit_logs l JOIN users u ON u.user_id=l.actor_id WHERE l.created_at>=? AND u.organization_id=? GROUP BY operation ORDER BY value DESC",(since_ms,org))
   type_dist=rows(c,f"SELECT entity_type name,count(*) value FROM audit_logs l JOIN users u ON u.user_id=l.actor_id WHERE l.created_at>=? AND u.organization_id=? GROUP BY entity_type ORDER BY value DESC",(since_ms,org))
   top_actors=rows(c,f"SELECT u.display_name name,count(*) value FROM audit_logs l JOIN users u ON u.user_id=l.actor_id WHERE l.created_at>=? AND u.organization_id=? GROUP BY l.actor_id ORDER BY value DESC LIMIT 10",(since_ms,org))
   daily=rows(c,f"SELECT date(l.created_at/1000,'unixepoch','localtime') day,count(*) value FROM audit_logs l JOIN users u ON u.user_id=l.actor_id WHERE l.created_at>=? AND u.organization_id=? GROUP BY day ORDER BY day",(since_ms,org))
   pack_dict=pack(its,total,pno,size)
   pack_dict.update({'heatMap':heat,'operationDistribution':op_dist,'entityTypeDistribution':type_dist,'topActors':top_actors,'dailyTrend':daily})
  return h.sendj(pack_dict) or True

 if root=='recycle' and ident and m=='POST':

   if not old:return bad(h,'NOT_FOUND','联系人不存在或未删除',404)

 if root=='recycle' and ident and m=='DELETE':
  if not (platform(db,a) or manage(db,a,'contact.delete',org)):return bad(h,'FORBIDDEN','无联系人永久删除权限',403)
  b=h.body();
  if str(b.get('confirm') or '')!='PURGE':return bad(h,'PURGE_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED','永久删除需要明确确认',428)
  try:
   with db.tx() as c:
    scope,scope_args,_=data_scope_clause_in_tx(c,a,'contact.read',org,alias='c');old=one(c,'SELECT c.* FROM contacts c WHERE c.contact_id=? AND c.organization_id=? AND c.deleted_at IS NOT NULL AND ('+scope+')',[ident,org]+scope_args)
    if not old:return bad(h,'NOT_FOUND','联系人不存在、未在回收站或不在授权范围',404)
    # 只清理数据库声明为直接引用 contacts 的行；事务保证任何遗漏引用都会整体回滚。
    tables=[r[0] for r in c.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'").fetchall()]
    deleted={}
    for table in tables:
     if table in ('contacts','audit_logs'):continue
     refs=[]
     try:
      for fk in c.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_list("'+table.replace('"','""')+'")').fetchall():
       if str(fk[2])=='contacts' and str(fk[4])=='contact_id':refs.append(str(fk[3]))
     except sqlite3.DatabaseError:continue
     # 兼容历史表未声明 FK 但使用标准 contact_id 字段的情况。
     cols=[str(x[1]) for x in c.execute('PRAGMA table_info("'+table.replace('"','""')+'")').fetchall()]
     if 'contact_id' in cols and 'contact_id' not in refs:refs.append('contact_id')
     if not refs:continue
     where=' OR '.join('"'+col.replace('"','""')+'"=?' for col in refs);cur=c.execute('DELETE FROM "'+table.replace('"','""')+'" WHERE '+where,[ident]*len(refs))
     if cur.rowcount:deleted[table]=cur.rowcount
    cur=c.execute('DELETE FROM contacts WHERE contact_id=? AND organization_id=? AND deleted_at IS NOT NULL',(ident,org))
    if cur.rowcount!=1:raise RuntimeError('CONTACT_PURGE_RACE')
    audit_in_tx(c,a,'purge','contact_tombstone',ident,{'contactId':ident,'workspaceId':old['workspace_id'],'deletedAt':old.get('deleted_at')},{'purged':True,'relatedRows':deleted},'回收站永久删除',old['workspace_id'])
   return h.sendj({'ok':True,'contactId':ident,'purgedRelatedRows':deleted}) or True
  except sqlite3.IntegrityError:return bad(h,'CONTACT_PURGE_BLOCKED','联系人仍被受保护业务记录引用，永久删除已整体回滚',409)

 if root=='field-definitions' and (m=='POST' or (ident and m=='PATCH')):
  if not manage(db,a,'contact.update',org):return bad(h,'FORBIDDEN','无字段定义管理权限',403)
  x=h.body();wid=str(x.get('workspace') or q.get('workspace') or '').strip();key=str(ident or x.get('fieldKey') or '').strip().lower();label=str(x.get('label') or '').strip();ftype=str(x.get('fieldType') or 'text')
  if ftype=='select':ftype='single_select'
  if not wid or not key or not label:return bad(h,'INVALID_INPUT','工作区、键名和标签不能为空')
  if not __import__('re').fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,80}',key):return bad(h,'INVALID_KEY','字段键名无效')
  if ftype not in ('text','number','date','boolean','json','single_select','multi_select'):return bad(h,'INVALID_TYPE','字段类型无效')
  opts=x.get('options',x.get('optionsJson',[]))
  if isinstance(opts,str):
   try:opts=json.loads(opts) if opts.strip().startswith('[') else [v.strip() for v in opts.split(',') if v.strip()]
   except Exception:return bad(h,'INVALID_OPTIONS','候选项格式无效')
  if not isinstance(opts,list) or any(not isinstance(v,str) or not v.strip() for v in opts) or len(opts)!=len(set(opts)):return bad(h,'INVALID_OPTIONS','候选项必须是不重复的非空字符串数组')
  opts=[v.strip()[:120] for v in opts]
  if ftype in ('single_select','multi_select') and not opts:return bad(h,'OPTIONS_REQUIRED','选择字段必须配置候选项')
  if ftype not in ('single_select','multi_select'):opts=[]
  n=now_ms()
  try:
   with db.tx() as c:
    if not c.execute('SELECT 1 FROM workspaces WHERE workspace_id=? AND organization_id=?',(wid,org)).fetchone():return bad(h,'WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND','工作区不存在',404)
    old=one(c,'SELECT * FROM custom_field_definitions WHERE workspace_id=? AND field_key=?',(wid,key))
    if m=='POST' and old:return bad(h,'FIELD_CONFLICT','字段键名已存在',409)
    if m=='PATCH' and not old:return bad(h,'NOT_FOUND','字段定义不存在',404)
    enabled=1 if x.get('enabled',old['enabled'] if old else True) not in (False,0,'0') else 0;quick=1 if x.get('isQuickEdit',old['is_quick_edit'] if old else False) not in (False,0,'0','') else 0
    role=str(x.get('fieldRole',old['field_role'] if old else 'custom'))[:80];zone=str(x.get('displayZone',old['display_zone'] if old else 'interaction_panel'))[:80];placeholder=str(x.get('placeholder',old['placeholder'] if old else ''))[:240];order=int(x.get('sortOrder',old['sort_order'] if old else 0))
    vals=(label,ftype,order,enabled,n,role,zone,quick,json.dumps(opts,ensure_ascii=False),placeholder)
    if m=='POST':c.execute('INSERT INTO custom_field_definitions(workspace_id,field_key,label,field_type,sort_order,enabled,created_at,updated_at,field_role,display_zone,is_quick_edit,options_json,placeholder) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(wid,key,label,ftype,order,enabled,n,n,role,zone,quick,json.dumps(opts,ensure_ascii=False),placeholder))
    else:c.execute('UPDATE custom_field_definitions SET label=?,field_type=?,sort_order=?,enabled=?,updated_at=?,field_role=?,display_zone=?,is_quick_edit=?,options_json=?,placeholder=? WHERE workspace_id=? AND field_key=?',vals+(wid,key))
    # 定义更新后立即协调已有值，插件与客户档案下一次读取即采用新定义。
    if m=='PATCH':
     value_rows=c.execute('SELECT v.contact_id,v.value_json FROM contact_custom_values v JOIN contacts ct ON ct.contact_id=v.contact_id WHERE ct.workspace_id=? AND v.field_key=?',(wid,key)).fetchall()
     allowed=set(opts)
     for vr in value_rows:
      try:value=json.loads(vr[1])
      except Exception:value=None
      if ftype=='multi_select':
       seq=value if isinstance(value,list) else ([] if value in (None,'') else [value]);clean=[]
       for v in seq:
        if isinstance(v,str) and v in allowed and v not in clean:clean.append(v)
       value=clean
      elif ftype=='single_select':value=value if isinstance(value,str) and value in allowed else None
      elif ftype in ('text','date'):value=value if isinstance(value,str) else ''
      elif ftype=='number':value=value if isinstance(value,(int,float)) and not isinstance(value,bool) else None
      elif ftype=='boolean':value=value if isinstance(value,bool) else None
      if value is None:c.execute('DELETE FROM contact_custom_values WHERE contact_id=? AND field_key=?',(vr[0],key))
      else:c.execute('UPDATE contact_custom_values SET value_json=?,updated_at=?,updated_by=? WHERE contact_id=? AND field_key=?',(json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':')),n,a['user_id'],vr[0],key))
    item=one(c,'SELECT * FROM custom_field_definitions WHERE workspace_id=? AND field_key=?',(wid,key));audit_in_tx(c,a,'create' if m=='POST' else 'update','field_definition',key,old or {},item,wid)
   return h.sendj({'ok':True,'item':item},201 if m=='POST' else 200) or True
  except (sqlite3.IntegrityError,ValueError):return bad(h,'FIELD_CONFLICT','字段配置冲突或排序无效',409)

def enroll_plugin(db,p,client_ip='',user_agent='',ttl_ms=30*24*3600*1000):
 """匿名核销一次性邀请码；在单事务内登记插件实例并签发受限会话。"""
 code=_normalize_code(p.get('invitationCode') or p.get('bindingCode') or p.get('binding_code'));iid=str(p.get('installationId') or p.get('installation_id') or '').strip()
 if len(code)<16 or len(iid)<16 or len(iid)>160:return _err('ENROLLMENT_INVALID','接入码或安装实例无效',400)
 digest=_hash(code);n=now_ms()
 with db.tx() as c:
  e=c.execute('SELECT * FROM device_enrollments WHERE binding_code_hash=?',(digest,)).fetchone()
  # 对外统一失败，避免泄漏邀请码的存在、状态和归属。
  if not e or e['status']!='pending':return _err('ENROLLMENT_INVALID','接入码无效、已使用或已撤销',409)
  if e['expires_at']<=n:
   c.execute("UPDATE device_enrollments SET status='expired' WHERE enrollment_id=? AND status='pending'",(e['enrollment_id'],))
   return _err('ENROLLMENT_INVALID','接入码无效、已使用或已撤销',409)
  uid=e['assigned_user_id']
  if not uid:return _err('ENROLLMENT_INVALID','接入邀请未指定用户',409)
  u=c.execute("""SELECT u.user_id,u.display_name,u.organization_id FROM users u
   JOIN team_memberships tm ON tm.user_id=u.user_id AND tm.team_id=? AND tm.status='active'
   WHERE u.user_id=? AND u.organization_id=? AND u.status='active'""",(e['team_id'],uid,e['organization_id'])).fetchone()
  t=c.execute("SELECT * FROM teams WHERE team_id=? AND organization_id=? AND status='active'",(e['team_id'],e['organization_id'])).fetchone()
  w=c.execute("SELECT * FROM workspaces WHERE workspace_id=? AND organization_id=? AND status='active'",(e['workspace_id'],e['organization_id'])).fetchone()
  relation=c.execute("SELECT 1 FROM workspace_teams WHERE workspace_id=? AND team_id=? AND organization_id=? AND status='active'",(e['workspace_id'],e['team_id'],e['organization_id'])).fetchone()
  if not u or not t or not w or (t['workspace_id']!=e['workspace_id'] and not relation):return _err('ENROLLMENT_SCOPE_INVALID','邀请归属已失效，请管理员重新创建',409)
  if c.execute('SELECT 1 FROM devices WHERE installation_id=?',(iid,)).fetchone():return _err('INSTALLATION_CONFLICT','该插件安装实例已登记；请先退出或联系管理员处理',409)
  did='dev_'+uuid.uuid4().hex;sid='pls_'+uuid.uuid4().hex;token='jbp_'+secrets.token_urlsafe(40);exp=n+min(max(int(ttl_ms),3600000),90*24*3600*1000)
  alias=str(p.get('deviceAlias') or '')[:120];computer=str(p.get('computerAlias') or '')[:120];client=str(p.get('clientVersion') or '')[:40]
  system_name=str(p.get('browser') or user_agent or 'Browser')[:120]
  metadata=dumps({'browser':str(p.get('browser') or '')[:80],'userAgent':str(user_agent)[:300],'clientIp':str(client_ip)[:80],'enrollmentId':e['enrollment_id']})
  c.execute("""INSERT INTO devices(device_id,workspace_id,installation_id,device_name,computer_name,status,first_seen_at,last_seen_at,metadata_json,organization_id,team_id,assigned_user_id,system_device_name,system_computer_name,user_device_alias,user_computer_alias,admin_device_name,admin_computer_name)
   VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,'active',?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",(did,e['workspace_id'],iid,alias or system_name,computer,n,n,metadata,e['organization_id'],e['team_id'],uid,system_name,computer,alias,computer,e['admin_device_name'] or '',e['admin_computer_name'] or ''))
  c.execute('INSERT INTO device_assignments(assignment_id,device_id,assigned_user_id,team_id,assigned_at,released_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,NULL)',('asn_'+secrets.token_hex(12),did,uid,e['team_id'],n))
  changed=c.execute("UPDATE device_enrollments SET status='consumed',consumed_at=?,consumed_device_id=? WHERE enrollment_id=? AND status='pending' AND expires_at>?",(n,did,e['enrollment_id'],n)).rowcount
  if changed!=1:raise RuntimeError('ENROLLMENT_RACE')
  c.execute("INSERT INTO plugin_sessions(plugin_session_id,organization_id,workspace_id,team_id,user_id,device_id,token_hash,status,issued_at,expires_at,last_seen_at,client_version,metadata_json) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'active',?,?,?,?,?)",(sid,e['organization_id'],e['workspace_id'],e['team_id'],uid,did,_hash(token),n,exp,n,client,dumps({'enrollmentId':e['enrollment_id']})))

def rebind_plugin(db,p,client_ip='',user_agent='',ttl_ms=30*24*3600*1000):
 """核销管理员签发的一次性换绑码；必须由原安装实例发起并保留原设备档案。"""
 code=_normalize_code(p.get('rebindCode') or p.get('bindingCode') or p.get('rebind_code'));iid=str(p.get('installationId') or p.get('installation_id') or '').strip()
 if len(code)<16 or len(iid)<16 or len(iid)>160:return _err('REBIND_INVALID','换绑码或安装实例无效',400)
 digest=_hash(code);n=now_ms()
 with db.tx() as c:
  r=c.execute('SELECT * FROM device_rebind_requests WHERE code_hash=?',(digest,)).fetchone()
  if not r or r['status']!='pending':return _err('REBIND_INVALID','换绑码无效、已使用或已撤销',409)
  if r['expires_at']<n:
   c.execute("UPDATE device_rebind_requests SET status='expired' WHERE rebind_id=? AND status='pending'",(r['rebind_id'],))
   return _err('REBIND_INVALID','换绑码无效、已使用或已撤销',409)
  d=c.execute('SELECT * FROM devices WHERE device_id=? AND organization_id=?',(r['device_id'],r['organization_id'])).fetchone()
  if not d or d['status']!='active' or d['installation_id']!=iid:return _err('REBIND_INSTANCE_MISMATCH','换绑码不属于当前插件安装实例',409)
  u=c.execute("SELECT u.user_id,u.display_name FROM users u JOIN team_memberships tm ON tm.user_id=u.user_id AND tm.team_id=? AND tm.status='active' WHERE u.user_id=? AND u.organization_id=? AND u.status='active'",(r['to_team_id'],r['to_user_id'],r['organization_id'])).fetchone()
  t=c.execute("SELECT * FROM teams WHERE team_id=? AND organization_id=? AND status='active'",(r['to_team_id'],r['organization_id'])).fetchone()
  w=c.execute("SELECT * FROM workspaces WHERE workspace_id=? AND organization_id=? AND status='active'",(r['to_workspace_id'],r['organization_id'])).fetchone()
  if not u or not t or not w or t['workspace_id']!=w['workspace_id']:return _err('REBIND_SCOPE_INVALID','目标用户、团队或工作区已失效',409)

  if changed!=1:return _err('REBIND_INVALID','换绑码已被使用',409)
  c.execute('UPDATE device_assignments SET released_at=? WHERE device_id=? AND released_at IS NULL',(n,d['device_id']))
  c.execute('INSERT INTO device_assignments(assignment_id,device_id,assigned_user_id,team_id,assigned_at,released_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,NULL)',('asn_'+secrets.token_hex(12),d['device_id'],r['to_user_id'],r['to_team_id'],n))
  c.execute('UPDATE devices SET workspace_id=?,team_id=?,assigned_user_id=?,last_seen_at=? WHERE device_id=?',(r['to_workspace_id'],r['to_team_id'],r['to_user_id'],n,d['device_id']))
  c.execute("UPDATE plugin_sessions SET status='revoked',revoked_at=?,revoked_by=?,revoked_reason='device_rebound' WHERE device_id=? AND status='active'",(n,r['created_by'],d['device_id']))
  token='jbp_'+secrets.token_urlsafe(40);sid='pls_'+uuid.uuid4().hex;exp=n+min(max(int(ttl_ms),3600000),90*24*3600*1000);client=str(p.get('clientVersion') or '')[:40]
  c.execute("INSERT INTO plugin_sessions(plugin_session_id,organization_id,workspace_id,team_id,user_id,device_id,token_hash,status,issued_at,expires_at,last_seen_at,client_version,metadata_json) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'active',?,?,?,?,?)",(sid,r['organization_id'],r['to_workspace_id'],r['to_team_id'],r['to_user_id'],d['device_id'],_hash(token),n,exp,n,client,dumps({'rebindId':r['rebind_id'],'clientIp':str(client_ip)[:80],'userAgent':str(user_agent)[:300]})))

def detect_account(db,a,p):
 with db.tx() as c:
  platform=_platform(c,p)
  if not platform:return _err('PLATFORM_NOT_FOUND','平台不存在或未启用',404)
  code=platform['platform_code'];typ,raw,canonical=normalize_identity(code,p.get('identityType'),p.get('identityValue'))
  if not canonical:return _err('STABLE_ACCOUNT_IDENTITY_REQUIRED','无法识别当前平台登录账号的稳定身份，已停止采集',409,platformCode=code,identityType=typ)
  n=now_ms();base="""SELECT DISTINCT ca.* FROM channel_account_identities i
   JOIN channel_accounts ca ON ca.channel_account_id=i.channel_account_id
   WHERE i.platform_id=? AND i.identity_type=? AND i.canonical_value=? AND i.status='active'
   AND (i.valid_from IS NULL OR i.valid_from<=?) AND (i.valid_to IS NULL OR i.valid_to>=?)
   AND ca.organization_id=? AND ca.platform_id=? AND ca.status='active'"""
  candidates=c.execute(base,(platform['platform_id'],typ,canonical,n,n,a['organization_id'],platform['platform_id'])).fetchall()
  detail={'platformCode':code,'identityType':typ,'identityValue':canonical,'adminAction':'请管理员审批当前平台身份，或为当前用户预分配唯一自动绑定槽位'}
  if len(candidates)>1:
   record_discovery_in_tx(c,a,platform,typ,raw,canonical,'conflict','identity_conflict')
   _record_identity_discovery(c,a,platform,typ,canonical,'identity_conflict')
   return _err('ACCOUNT_IDENTITY_CONFLICT','平台账号身份存在重复登记，请联系管理员处理',409,matches=len(candidates),**detail)
  account=candidates[0] if candidates else None
  authorized=False
  if account:
   team=c.execute('SELECT 1 FROM team_channel_accounts WHERE team_id=? AND channel_account_id=?',(a['team_id'],account['channel_account_id'])).fetchone()
   user=c.execute("SELECT 1 FROM user_channel_account_grants WHERE user_id=? AND channel_account_id=?",(a['user_id'],account['channel_account_id'])).fetchone()
   admin=c.execute('SELECT 1 FROM platform_administrators WHERE user_id=?',(a['user_id'],)).fetchone()
   authorized=bool(team and (user or admin))
  if not authorized:
   result=discover_or_auto_claim_in_tx(c,a,platform,typ,raw,canonical,account['channel_account_id'] if account else None)
   if result['outcome']=='conflict':
    _record_identity_discovery(c,a,platform,typ,canonical,result['reason'])
    return _err('ACCOUNT_IDENTITY_CONFLICT','平台账号身份或预分配槽位存在冲突，请联系管理员处理',409,**detail)
   if result['outcome']!='claimed':
    _record_identity_discovery(c,a,platform,typ,canonical,result['reason'])
    return _err('ACCOUNT_APPROVAL_PENDING','已检测到当前平台账号，正在等待管理员审批',403,discoveryId=result['discovery']['discovery_id'],reasonCode=result['reason'],**detail)
   account=result['account']
  bid='dcb_'+uuid.uuid4().hex
  c.execute("""INSERT INTO device_channel_account_bindings(binding_id,organization_id,workspace_id,team_id,device_id,user_id,channel_account_id,platform_id,external_account_key,status,bound_at,bound_by,last_verified_at,metadata_json)
   VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'active',?,?,?,'{}') ON CONFLICT(device_id,platform_id,external_account_key) DO UPDATE SET
   organization_id=excluded.organization_id,workspace_id=excluded.workspace_id,team_id=excluded.team_id,user_id=excluded.user_id,channel_account_id=excluded.channel_account_id,status='active',last_verified_at=excluded.last_verified_at""",(bid,a['organization_id'],a['workspace_id'],a['team_id'],a['device_id'],a['user_id'],account['channel_account_id'],platform['platform_id'],canonical,n,a['user_id'],n))
  return _ok(matchStatus='matched',platformCode=code,identityType=typ,identityValue=canonical,channelAccount={'channelAccountId':account['channel_account_id'],'displayName':account['display_name'],'businessPurpose':account['business_purpose'],'businessScene':account['business_scene'],'contactEntryPolicy':account['contact_entry_policy'],'collectionEnabled':bool(account['collection_enabled'])})

def issue_plugin_session(db,actor,p,ttl_ms=30*24*3600*1000):

=== V208_collector_service.py:321-334 plugin_actor [static evidence only] ===
def plugin_actor(db,token,touch=False):
 if not token:return None
 n=now_ms()
 with db.tx(touch) as c:
  row=c.execute("""SELECT p.*,u.status user_status,d.status device_status,d.assigned_user_id,d.team_id device_team_id,d.organization_id device_org_id,
   EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM team_memberships tm WHERE tm.user_id=p.user_id AND tm.team_id=p.team_id AND tm.status='active') team_member,
   EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM platform_administrators pa WHERE pa.user_id=p.user_id) platform_admin
   FROM plugin_sessions p JOIN users u ON u.user_id=p.user_id JOIN devices d ON d.device_id=p.device_id WHERE p.token_hash=?""",(_hash(token),)).fetchone()
  if not row or row['status']!='active' or row['expires_at']<n or row['user_status']!='active' or row['device_status']!='active':return None
  if row['assigned_user_id']!=row['user_id'] or row['device_team_id']!=row['team_id'] or row['device_org_id']!=row['organization_id'] or not (row['team_member'] or row['platform_admin']):return None
  if touch:
   c.execute('UPDATE plugin_sessions SET last_seen_at=? WHERE plugin_session_id=?',(n,row['plugin_session_id']))
   c.execute('UPDATE devices SET last_seen_at=? WHERE device_id=?',(n,row['device_id']))
  return dict(row)

def heartbeat(db,a):

def list_channel_accounts(db,a,p=None):
 p=p or {}

  if (p.get('platformId') or p.get('platformCode')) and not platform:return _err('PLATFORM_NOT_FOUND','平台不存在或未启用',404)
  params=[a['organization_id'],a['team_id'],a['user_id'],a['user_id']]
  extra=''
  if platform:extra=' AND x.platform_id=?';params.append(platform['platform_id'])
  rows=c.execute("""SELECT DISTINCT x.channel_account_id,x.display_name,x.channel,x.platform_id,p.platform_code,x.business_purpose,x.business_scene,x.contact_entry_policy,x.collection_enabled
   FROM channel_accounts x JOIN team_channel_accounts t ON t.channel_account_id=x.channel_account_id

def resolve_follow_ups(db,a,p):
 """Resolve visible conversations without assuming that WA @c.us and @lid are the same key."""
 if not isinstance(p,dict):return _err('INVALID_REQUEST','请求正文必须是对象')
 code=str(p.get('platformCode') or '').strip().lower()
 aid=str(p.get('channelAccountId') or '').strip()
 raw=p.get('externalConversationIds')
 if code not in ('whatsapp','telegram','instagram','messenger','facebook'):
  return _err('PLATFORM_REQUIRED','platformCode 无效')
 if not aid:return _err('CHANNEL_ACCOUNT_REQUIRED','channelAccountId 必填')
 if not isinstance(raw,list):return _err('CONVERSATION_IDS_REQUIRED','externalConversationIds 必须是数组')
 ids=[]
 for value in raw:
  value=str(value or '').strip()
  if not value or len(value)>500:continue
  if value not in ids:ids.append(value)
 if not ids:return _ok(items=[])
 if len(ids)>100:return _err('TOO_MANY_CONVERSATIONS','单次最多解析 100 个会话')
 with db.tx(False) as c:
  platform=_platform(c,{'platformCode':code})
  if not platform:return _err('PLATFORM_NOT_FOUND','平台未启用',404)
  account=_account_allowed(c,a,aid,platform['platform_id'])
  if not account:return _err('ACCOUNT_NOT_AUTHORIZED','渠道账号未授权',403)
  prefix={'instagram':'ig','whatsapp':'wa','telegram':'tg','facebook':'facebook','messenger':'messenger'}[code]+':'
  request_aliases={}
  alias_owner={}
  for requested in ids:
   token=requested.split(':',1)[1] if requested.startswith(prefix) else requested
   values=[]
   for candidate in (requested,token,prefix+token):
    if candidate and candidate not in values:values.append(candidate)
   request_aliases[requested]=values
   for candidate in values:alias_owner.setdefault(candidate,[]).append(requested)
  all_aliases=list(alias_owner)
  marks=','.join('?' for _ in all_aliases)
  base_params=(a['organization_id'],a['workspace_id'],a['team_id'],aid,platform['platform_id'])
  exact_rows=c.execute(f"""SELECT v.external_conversation_id,v.conversation_id,v.contact_id,
    ct.next_follow_up_at,ct.business_json,ct.version,v.updated_at
   FROM conversations v JOIN contacts ct ON ct.contact_id=v.contact_id
   WHERE v.organization_id=? AND v.workspace_id=? AND v.team_id=?
    AND v.channel_account_id=? AND v.platform_id=?
    AND ct.deleted_at IS NULL AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM follow_up_notifications fn WHERE fn.contact_id=ct.contact_id AND fn.follow_up_at=ct.next_follow_up_at AND fn.status='completed') AND v.external_conversation_id IN ({marks})
   ORDER BY v.updated_at DESC,v.conversation_id""",(*base_params,*all_aliases)).fetchall()
  matches={}
  methods={}
  if code=='whatsapp':
   alias_rows=c.execute(f'''SELECT x.alias_value,v.external_conversation_id,v.conversation_id,v.contact_id,ct.next_follow_up_at,ct.business_json,ct.version,v.updated_at
    FROM conversation_external_aliases x JOIN conversations v ON v.conversation_id=x.conversation_id JOIN contacts ct ON ct.contact_id=v.contact_id
    WHERE x.organization_id=? AND x.workspace_id=? AND x.channel_account_id=? AND x.platform_id=? AND x.alias_value IN ({marks})
     AND v.team_id=? AND ct.deleted_at IS NULL AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM follow_up_notifications fn WHERE fn.contact_id=ct.contact_id AND fn.follow_up_at=ct.next_follow_up_at AND fn.status='completed') ORDER BY v.updated_at DESC''',(a['organization_id'],a['workspace_id'],aid,platform['platform_id'],*all_aliases,a['team_id'])).fetchall()
   for row in alias_rows:
    for requested in alias_owner.get(row['alias_value'],()):
     if requested not in matches:matches[requested]=row;methods[requested]='conversation_external_alias'
  for row in exact_rows:
   for requested in alias_owner.get(row['external_conversation_id'],()):
    if requested not in matches:
     matches[requested]=row;methods[requested]='external_conversation_id'
  # WhatsApp Web may expose a phone JID while an existing conversation is keyed by LID
  # (or vice versa). Resolve that mismatch only through an identity already bound to the
  # same contact; never infer equivalence from the unrelated numeric local parts.
  if code=='whatsapp':
   identity_requests={}
   for requested in ids:
    if requested in matches:continue
    value=requested.removeprefix('wa:').removeprefix('whatsapp:')
    typ='whatsapp_lid' if value.lower().endswith('@lid') else 'whatsapp_jid'
    normalized_type,_,canonical=normalize_identity('whatsapp',typ,value)
    if canonical:identity_requests.setdefault((normalized_type,canonical),[]).append(requested)
   if identity_requests:
    canonicals=list(dict.fromkeys(x[1] for x in identity_requests))
    imarks=','.join('?' for _ in canonicals)
    identities=c.execute(f"""SELECT contact_id,identity_type,canonical_value
     FROM contact_identities WHERE organization_id=? AND workspace_id=? AND platform_id=?
      AND verification_status IN ('unverified','observed','verified') AND canonical_value IN ({imarks})""",
     (a['organization_id'],a['workspace_id'],platform['platform_id'],*canonicals)).fetchall()
    contact_requests={}
    for identity in identities:
     for requested in identity_requests.get((identity['identity_type'],identity['canonical_value']),()):
      contact_requests.setdefault(identity['contact_id'],[]).append(requested)
    if contact_requests:
     contact_ids=list(contact_requests)
     cmarks=','.join('?' for _ in contact_ids)
     alias_rows=c.execute(f"""SELECT v.external_conversation_id,v.conversation_id,v.contact_id,
       ct.next_follow_up_at,ct.business_json,ct.version,v.updated_at
      FROM conversations v JOIN contacts ct ON ct.contact_id=v.contact_id
      WHERE v.organization_id=? AND v.workspace_id=? AND v.team_id=?
       AND v.channel_account_id=? AND v.platform_id=?
       AND ct.deleted_at IS NULL AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM follow_up_notifications fn WHERE fn.contact_id=ct.contact_id AND fn.follow_up_at=ct.next_follow_up_at AND fn.status='completed') AND v.contact_id IN ({cmarks})
      ORDER BY v.updated_at DESC,v.conversation_id""",(*base_params,*contact_ids)).fetchall()
     for row in alias_rows:
      for requested in contact_requests.get(row['contact_id'],()):
       if requested not in matches:
        matches[requested]=row;methods[requested]='contact_identity_alias'
     # A trusted visible-row identity may belong to a contact that has not yet created a
     # conversation under this channel account.  Resolve the follow-up directly, but only
     # inside the actor's organization/workspace and team (or explicit team-share) scope.
     unresolved_contacts={cid:reqs for cid,reqs in contact_requests.items() if any(x not in matches for x in reqs)}
     if unresolved_contacts:
      direct_ids=list(unresolved_contacts)
      dmarks=','.join('?' for _ in direct_ids)
      direct_rows=c.execute(f"""SELECT ct.contact_id,ct.next_follow_up_at,ct.business_json,ct.version,ct.updated_at
       FROM contacts ct WHERE ct.organization_id=? AND ct.workspace_id=? AND ct.deleted_at IS NULL AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM follow_up_notifications fn WHERE fn.contact_id=ct.contact_id AND fn.follow_up_at=ct.next_follow_up_at AND fn.status='completed')
        AND ct.contact_id IN ({dmarks})
        AND (ct.team_id=? OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM contact_team_access x
         WHERE x.organization_id=ct.organization_id AND x.workspace_id=ct.workspace_id
          AND x.contact_id=ct.contact_id AND x.team_id=? AND x.revoked_at IS NULL))""",
       (a['organization_id'],a['workspace_id'],*direct_ids,a['team_id'],a['team_id'])).fetchall()
      for contact in direct_rows:
       for requested in unresolved_contacts.get(contact['contact_id'],()):
        if requested in matches:continue
        row=dict(contact);row.update({'external_conversation_id':requested,'conversation_id':None})
        matches[requested]=row;methods[requested]='contact_identity'
  out=[]
  for requested in ids:
   row=matches.get(requested)
   if not row or row['next_follow_up_at'] is None:continue
   try:reason=str((json.loads(row['business_json'] or '{}') or {}).get('nextFollowUpReason') or '')[:500]
   except Exception:reason=''
   canonical=row['external_conversation_id']
   out.append({'externalConversationId':requested,'requestedExternalConversationId':requested,
    'canonicalExternalConversationId':canonical,'aliases':list(dict.fromkeys([requested,canonical])),
    'matchedBy':methods.get(requested,'external_conversation_id'),'conversationId':row['conversation_id'],
    'contactId':row['contact_id'],'nextFollowUpAt':int(row['next_follow_up_at']),
    'nextFollowUpReason':reason,'contactVersion':int(row['version'] or 0)})
  return _ok(items=out)

def collection_state(db,a,conversation_id):
 with db.tx(False) as c:
  row=c.execute("SELECT * FROM conversations WHERE conversation_id=? AND organization_id=? AND workspace_id=? AND team_id=? AND assigned_user_id=?",(conversation_id,a['organization_id'],a['workspace_id'],a['team_id'],a['user_id'])).fetchone()
  if not row:return _err('CONVERSATION_SCOPE_INVALID','会话不存在或不属于当前采集范围',404)
  if not _account_allowed(c,a,row['channel_account_id'],row['platform_id']):return _err('ACCOUNT_NOT_AUTHORIZED','渠道账号未授权',403)
  if not c.execute("SELECT 1 FROM device_channel_account_bindings WHERE device_id=? AND user_id=? AND channel_account_id=? AND platform_id=? AND status='active'",(a['device_id'],a['user_id'],row['channel_account_id'],row['platform_id'])).fetchone():return _err('ACCOUNT_BINDING_REQUIRED','当前设备未绑定该渠道账号',403)
  generation,meta=_collection_generation(row)
  return _ok(conversationId=conversation_id,collectionGeneration=generation,messageCount=int(row['message_count'] or 0),recollectRequestedAt=meta.get('recollectRequestedAt'))

def collect_messages(db,a,p):
 if not isinstance(p,dict):return _err('INVALID_REQUEST','请求正文必须是对象')
 items=p.get('messages')
 if not isinstance(items,list) or not items or len(items)>100:return _err('INVALID_BATCH','messages 必须是 1 至 100 条的数组')
 if len(json.dumps(p,ensure_ascii=False).encode())>524288:return _err('REQUEST_TOO_LARGE','消息批次超过 512KB',413)
 out=[]
 for item in items:
  if not isinstance(item,dict):out.append({'ok':False,'code':'MESSAGE_INVALID'});continue
  try:out.append(_collect_one(db,a,item))
  except Exception as ex:
   LOG.exception('collector message write failed: %s',str(ex)[:200])
   out.append({'ok':False,'code':'MESSAGE_INTERNAL_ERROR','detail':str(ex)[:300]})
 duplicated=sum(bool(x.get('duplicate')) for x in out);accepted=sum(bool(x.get('ok')) and not x.get('duplicate') for x in out)
 rejected_codes={}
 for x in out:
  if not x.get('ok'):
   code=str(x.get('code') or 'UNKNOWN');rejected_codes[code]=rejected_codes.get(code,0)+1
 translation_received=sum(bool((x.get('metadata') or {}).get('translation')) for x in items if isinstance(x,dict) and isinstance(x.get('metadata'),dict))
 translation_enriched=sum(bool(x.get('translationEnriched')) for x in out)
 wa_items=[x for x in items if isinstance(x,dict) and str((x.get('metadata') or {}).get('platformCode') or '').lower()=='whatsapp']
 if wa_items:
  import hashlib
  conversation_fingerprints=sorted(set(hashlib.sha256(str(x.get('conversationId') or 'unregistered').encode()).hexdigest()[:12] for x in wa_items))[:10]
  probe_counts=[int(((x.get('metadata') or {}).get('waTranslationDiagnostic') or {}).get('probeTranslationCount') or 0) for x in wa_items]
  normalized_counts=[int(((x.get('metadata') or {}).get('waTranslationDiagnostic') or {}).get('normalizedTranslationCount') or 0) for x in wa_items]
  LOG.info('WA_TRANSLATION_DIAGNOSTIC version=210.2.75 conversations=%s messages=%d received=%d probeMax=%d normalizedMax=%d enriched=%d accepted=%d duplicated=%d rejected=%d',','.join(conversation_fingerprints),len(wa_items),translation_received,max(probe_counts or [0]),max(normalized_counts or [0]),translation_enriched,accepted,duplicated,max(0,len(out)-accepted-duplicated))
 elif translation_received:
  LOG.info('collector translation batch received=%d enriched=%d accepted=%d duplicated=%d',translation_received,translation_enriched,accepted,duplicated)
 if rejected_codes:LOG.warning('collector batch accepted=%d duplicated=%d rejected=%s',accepted,duplicated,json.dumps(rejected_codes,sort_keys=True))
 return _ok(results=out,accepted=accepted,duplicated=duplicated,rejected=len(out)-accepted-duplicated,total=len(out))

=== V208_contact_center_service.py:148-170 _can_any_scope [static evidence only] ===
def _can_any_scope(db,actor,permission,org):
 """Return whether an actor has a permission in any active RBAC scope in this organization."""
 if not actor or actor.get('organization_id')!=org:return False
 if is_platform_admin(db,actor):return True
 with db.tx(False) as c:
  bindings=_rows(c,"""SELECT DISTINCT b.scope_type,b.scope_id
   FROM role_bindings b
   JOIN roles r ON r.role_id=b.role_id AND r.status='active'
   JOIN role_permissions rp ON rp.role_id=b.role_id
   JOIN permissions p ON p.permission_key=rp.permission_key
   WHERE b.user_id=? AND b.organization_id=? AND b.status='active' AND rp.permission_key=?""",
   (actor['user_id'],org,permission))
 for b in bindings:
  typ,sid=b['scope_type'],b['scope_id']
  kw={'organization_id':org}
  if typ=='organization':pass
  elif typ=='workspace':kw['workspace_id']=sid
  elif typ=='department':kw['department_id']=sid
  elif typ=='team':kw['team_id']=sid
  elif typ=='self':kw['resource_user_id']=sid
  else:continue
  if can(db,actor,permission,**kw):return True
 return False

def follow_up_reminders(db,actor,org,q):
 """Return every visible overdue follow-up; never derives reminders from a list page."""
 delay=_int(q.get('delay_minutes') or q.get('delayMinutes'),10,0,1440)

 with db.tx(False) as c:
  scope,sp,scope_name=_scope(c,actor)
  where="c.organization_id=? AND "+scope+" AND c.deleted_at IS NULL AND c.next_follow_up_at IS NOT NULL AND c.next_follow_up_at<=? AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM conversation_messages fm WHERE fm.contact_id=c.contact_id AND fm.direction='outbound' AND fm.sender_type='agent' AND fm.status='active' AND COALESCE(fm.occurred_at,fm.collected_at)>=c.next_follow_up_at)"
  rows=_rows(c,"SELECT c.contact_id,c.manual_name,c.display_name,c.next_follow_up_at,c.business_json,c.version FROM contacts c WHERE "+where+" ORDER BY c.next_follow_up_at,c.contact_id",[org]+sp+[cutoff])

def _handle_transfers(h,m,seg,q,actor,db,org):

  return _send(h,*create_transfer(db,scoped,str(body.get('contactId') or ''),body))

   if not manageable:

    if not own or not row or row['requested_by']!=actor['user_id']:return _bad(h,'FORBIDDEN','只能取消本人发起的待处理转交',403)
  elif action in ('fail','expire') and not (is_platform_admin(db,actor) or can(db,actor,'transfer.manage',workspace_id=scoped['workspace_id'],team_id=scoped['team_id'],organization_id=org)):
   return _bad(h,'FORBIDDEN','无转交管理权限',403)
  return _send(h,*transition_transfer(db,scoped,seg[2],action,body))

def save_notification_subscriptions(db,actor,organization_id,payload):

def poll_notifications(db,actor,payload,organization_id=None):
 payload=payload if isinstance(payload,dict) else {};uid=actor['user_id'];org=str(organization_id or actor['organization_id']);n=now_ms();limit=_bounded_int(payload.get('limit'),100,1,100);ctype=str(payload.get('consumerType') or 'admin')[:30];cid=str(payload.get('consumerId') or '')[:160] or 'anonymous';pref=None;repeat=0
 with db.tx() as c:
  pref=_reminder_preferences(c,org,uid);repeat=pref['repeatMinutes']*60000
  rows=c.execute("""SELECT f.*,f.status notification_status,f.version notification_version,r.status receipt_status,r.alert_count,r.last_alerted_at,r.snooze_until,u.display_name owner_name
   FROM follow_up_notification_receipts r JOIN follow_up_notifications f ON f.notification_id=r.notification_id LEFT JOIN users u ON u.user_id=f.owner_user_id
   WHERE r.organization_id=? AND r.recipient_user_id=? AND f.status IN ('due','overdue') AND r.status NOT IN ('dismissed','closed')
   ORDER BY f.follow_up_at,f.notification_id LIMIT ?""",(org,uid,limit)).fetchall()
  out=[]
  for raw in rows:
   r=dict(raw);eligible=int(r.get('next_alert_at') or 0)<=n and int(r.get('snooze_until') or 0)<=n
   claimed=False
   if eligible:
    cur=c.execute("""UPDATE follow_up_notification_receipts SET status='alerted',first_alerted_at=COALESCE(first_alerted_at,?),last_alerted_at=?,next_alert_at=?,alert_count=alert_count+1,claimed_by_consumer=?,claim_expires_at=?,last_consumer_type=?,last_consumer_id=?,version=version+1,updated_at=?
     WHERE notification_id=? AND recipient_user_id=? AND status NOT IN ('dismissed','closed') AND COALESCE(next_alert_at,0)<=? AND COALESCE(snooze_until,0)<=? AND (claim_expires_at IS NULL OR claim_expires_at<=? OR claimed_by_consumer=?)""",(n,n,n+repeat,ctype+':'+cid,n+60000,ctype,cid,n,r['notification_id'],uid,n,n,n,ctype+':'+cid))
    claimed=cur.rowcount==1
    if claimed:r.update({'receipt_status':'alerted','alert_count':int(r['alert_count'] or 0)+1,'last_alerted_at':n})
   r['should_alert']=claimed;out.append(_dto(r,n))
  unread=c.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM follow_up_notification_receipts r JOIN follow_up_notifications f ON f.notification_id=r.notification_id WHERE r.organization_id=? AND r.recipient_user_id=? AND f.status IN ('due','overdue') AND r.status NOT IN ('dismissed','closed')",(org,uid)).fetchone()[0]
 return ({'ok':True,'serverTime':n,'unreadCount':unread,'preferences':pref,'items':out},200)

def list_notifications(db,actor,query=None,organization_id=None):
 query=query or {};org=str(organization_id or actor['organization_id']);uid=actor['user_id'];state=_query_scalar(query,'status','open');view=_query_scalar(query,'view','mine');receipt_state=_query_scalar(query,'receiptStatus','');search=_query_scalar(query,'q','')[:100];page=_bounded_int(_query_scalar(query,'page','1'),1,1,100000);size=_bounded_int(_query_scalar(query,'pageSize','50'),50,1,100)
 states=('scheduled','due','overdue') if state=='open' else (('scheduled','due','overdue','completed','cancelled','superseded') if state=='all' else ((state,) if state in ('scheduled','due','overdue','completed','cancelled','superseded') else ('scheduled','due','overdue')))
 marks=','.join('?' for _ in states);n=now_ms()
 with db.tx(False) as c:
  management,teams=_management_scope(c,actor,org);team_view=view in ('team','organization') and management!='personal'
  where=["f.organization_id=?",f"f.status IN ({marks})"];args=[org,*states]
  if team_view:
   if management=='teams':
    tmarks=','.join('?' for _ in teams);where.append(f"f.team_id IN ({tmarks})");args.extend(teams)
   join="LEFT JOIN follow_up_notification_receipts r ON r.notification_id=f.notification_id AND r.recipient_user_id=?";join_args=[uid]
  else:
   join="JOIN follow_up_notification_receipts r ON r.notification_id=f.notification_id AND r.recipient_user_id=?";join_args=[uid]
  if receipt_state in ('unread','alerted','read','snoozed','dismissed','closed'):where.append("r.status=?");args.append(receipt_state)
  if search:where.append("(f.customer_name LIKE ? OR f.follow_up_reason LIKE ? OR f.contact_id LIKE ?)");like='%'+search+'%';args.extend([like,like,like])
  condition=' AND '.join(where);params=join_args+args
  total=c.execute(f"SELECT count(*) FROM follow_up_notifications f {join} WHERE {condition}",params).fetchone()[0]
  rows=c.execute(f"""SELECT f.*,f.status notification_status,f.version notification_version,COALESCE(r.status,'') receipt_status,COALESCE(r.alert_count,0) alert_count,r.last_alerted_at,r.snooze_until,u.display_name owner_name,0 should_alert,CASE WHEN r.receipt_id IS NULL OR r.status='closed' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END can_act,(SELECT count(*) FROM follow_up_notification_receipts rr WHERE rr.notification_id=f.notification_id AND rr.status!='closed') recipient_count
   FROM follow_up_notifications f {join} LEFT JOIN users u ON u.user_id=f.owner_user_id WHERE {condition} ORDER BY CASE f.status WHEN 'overdue' THEN 0 WHEN 'due' THEN 1 WHEN 'scheduled' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END,f.follow_up_at DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?""",params+[size,(page-1)*size]).fetchall()
 return ({'ok':True,'view':'team' if team_view else 'mine','managementScope':management,'page':page,'pageSize':size,'total':total,'items':[_dto(dict(x),n) for x in rows]},200)

def notification_summary(db,actor,organization_id=None):
 org=str(organization_id or actor['organization_id']);uid=actor['user_id'];n=now_ms();start=n-(n%86400000)
 with db.tx(False) as c:
  counts={x['status']:x['n'] for x in c.execute("SELECT f.status,count(*) n FROM follow_up_notification_receipts r JOIN follow_up_notifications f ON f.notification_id=r.notification_id WHERE r.organization_id=? AND r.recipient_user_id=? GROUP BY f.status",(org,uid))}
  unread=c.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM follow_up_notification_receipts r JOIN follow_up_notifications f ON f.notification_id=r.notification_id WHERE r.organization_id=? AND r.recipient_user_id=? AND f.status IN ('due','overdue') AND r.status IN ('unread','alerted','snoozed','read')",(org,uid)).fetchone()[0]
  completed_today=c.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM follow_up_notification_receipts r JOIN follow_up_notifications f ON f.notification_id=r.notification_id WHERE r.organization_id=? AND r.recipient_user_id=? AND f.status='completed' AND f.completed_at>=?",(org,uid,start)).fetchone()[0]
  management,teams=_management_scope(c,actor,org);scope_sql='';scope_args=[]
  if management=='teams':scope_sql=' AND f.team_id IN ('+','.join('?' for _ in teams)+')';scope_args=teams
  if management=='personal':team_due=unread;unassigned=0
  else:
   team_due=c.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM follow_up_notifications f WHERE f.organization_id=? AND f.status IN ('due','overdue')"+scope_sql,[org,*scope_args]).fetchone()[0]
   unassigned=c.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM follow_up_notifications f WHERE f.organization_id=? AND f.status IN ('scheduled','due','overdue') AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM follow_up_notification_receipts r WHERE r.notification_id=f.notification_id AND r.status!='closed')"+scope_sql,[org,*scope_args]).fetchone()[0]
 return ({'ok':True,'scheduled':counts.get('scheduled',0),'due':counts.get('due',0),'overdue':counts.get('overdue',0),'completedToday':completed_today,'unread':unread,'teamDue':team_due,'unassigned':unassigned,'managementScope':management},200)

=== V208_follow_up_notification_service.py:329-346 receipt_action [static evidence only] ===
def receipt_action(db,actor,nid,action,payload=None,organization_id=None):
 payload=payload if isinstance(payload,dict) else {};uid=actor['user_id'];org=str(organization_id or actor['organization_id']);n=now_ms()
 with db.tx() as c:
  row=c.execute("SELECT f.*,r.status receipt_status,r.version receipt_version FROM follow_up_notifications f JOIN follow_up_notification_receipts r ON r.notification_id=f.notification_id WHERE f.notification_id=? AND f.organization_id=? AND r.recipient_user_id=?",(nid,org,uid)).fetchone()
  if not row or row['receipt_status']=='closed':return ({'ok':False,'code':'NOTIFICATION_NOT_FOUND','message':'通知不存在或无权处理'},404)
  before={'notificationStatus':row['status'],'receiptStatus':row['receipt_status'],'receiptVersion':row['receipt_version']}
  if action=='complete':
   changed=complete_follow_up_in_tx(c,row['contact_id'],n,uid,None,str(payload.get('completionMode') or 'manual')[:40],{'note':str(payload.get('note') or '')[:500]}) if row['status'] in OPEN else 0
   final='completed' if changed else row['status'];_audit(c,uid,row['workspace_id'],'follow-up.complete',nid,before,{'notificationStatus':final},str(payload.get('note') or '')[:500] or None)
   return ({'ok':True,'notificationId':nid,'status':final,'unchanged':not bool(changed)},200)
  if row['status'] not in OPEN:return ({'ok':True,'notificationId':nid,'status':row['status'],'unchanged':True},200)
  if action=='snooze':
   minutes=_reminder_preferences(c,org,uid)['snoozeMinutes'];until=n+minutes*60000;c.execute("UPDATE follow_up_notification_receipts SET status='snoozed',snooze_until=?,next_alert_at=?,claimed_by_consumer=NULL,claim_expires_at=NULL,version=version+1,updated_at=? WHERE notification_id=? AND recipient_user_id=?",(until,until,n,nid,uid));state='snoozed';after={'receiptStatus':state,'snoozeUntil':until}
  elif action=='dismiss':c.execute("UPDATE follow_up_notification_receipts SET status='dismissed',dismissed_at=?,claimed_by_consumer=NULL,claim_expires_at=NULL,version=version+1,updated_at=? WHERE notification_id=? AND recipient_user_id=?",(n,n,nid,uid));state='dismissed';after={'receiptStatus':state}
  elif action=='read':c.execute("UPDATE follow_up_notification_receipts SET status='read',read_at=COALESCE(read_at,?),version=version+1,updated_at=? WHERE notification_id=? AND recipient_user_id=? AND status NOT IN ('dismissed','closed')",(n,n,nid,uid));state='read';after={'receiptStatus':state}
  else:return ({'ok':False,'code':'INVALID_NOTIFICATION_ACTION','message':'不支持的通知操作'},400)
  _audit(c,uid,row['workspace_id'],'follow-up.'+action,nid,before,after)
 return ({'ok':True,'notificationId':nid,'status':state},200)

=== V208_follow_up_notification_service.py:348-358 start_scanner [static evidence only] ===
def start_scanner(db,interval=15):
 key=str(getattr(db,'path',getattr(db,'db_path',id(db))))
 with _SCANNER_LOCK:
  current=_SCANNERS.get(key)
  if current and current.is_alive():return current
  def run():
   while True:
    try:scan_due_notifications(db)
    except Exception:LOG.exception('follow-up notification scan failed')
    time.sleep(max(5,int(interval)))
  t=threading.Thread(target=run,name='follow-up-notification-scanner',daemon=True);_SCANNERS[key]=t;t.start();return t
